If you cooperate, you pay cost c for each of your neighbors. If you defect, you pay nothing but also give nothing to your neighbors. Your reward = b * (number of cooperating neighbors) - c * (your number of neighbors if you cooperate, 0 if you defect). Your goal is to maximize your own cumulative reward over many rounds. Each round you will see the individual actions of each of your neighbors (who cooperated, who defected) and your own past actions and rewards. However, I also paid a cost of c for each of my neighbors, which suggests that c is also relatively high.

Given this information, I'll assume that 2b - 3c is likely to be positive, which means it's better to cooperate. Therefore, I'll choose to cooperate.

</think>
